+void updatePivotRows(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivot,
+                     const Partition columns,
+                     const size_t localPivotRow,
+                     const size_t pivotBegin,
+                     const size_t blockSize) noexcept {
+    if (columns.count == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    size_t leftEnd = columns.count;
+    size_t rightBegin = columns.count;
+    if (pivotBegin >= columns.begin && pivotBegin < columns.begin + columns.count) {
+        leftEnd = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+        rightBegin = leftEnd + blockSize;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t q = 0; q < blockSize; ++q) {
+        const unsigned int* const qRow = dist.data()
+                                       + (localPivotRow + q) * columns.count;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+            if (i == q) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            unsigned int* const row = dist.data()
+                                    + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count;
+            const unsigned int leftDistance = pivot[i * blockSize + q];
+            updateContiguous(row, qRow, leftDistance, 0, leftEnd);
+            updateContiguous(row, qRow, leftDistance, rightBegin, columns.count);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updatePivotColumns(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivot,
+                        const Partition rows,
+                        const Partition columns,
+                        const size_t localPivotColumn,
+                        const size_t pivotBegin,
+                        const size_t blockSize) noexcept {
+    for (size_t q = 0; q < blockSize; ++q) {
+        const unsigned int* const pivotRow = pivot.data() + q * blockSize;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+            if (globalRow >= pivotBegin && globalRow < pivotBegin + blockSize) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            unsigned int* const row = dist.data() + i * columns.count + localPivotColumn;
+            const unsigned int leftDistance = row[q];
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < blockSize; ++j) {
+                const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + pivotRow[j];
+                if (candidate < row[j]) {
+                    row[j] = candidate;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
-bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Basic sanity checks
-    
-    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
-            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
-            return false;
+void updateRemaining(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRows,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumns,
+                     const Partition rows,
+                     const Partition columns,
+                     const size_t pivotBegin,
+                     const size_t blockSize) noexcept {
+    if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    constexpr size_t COLUMN_TILE = 256;
+    size_t leftEnd = columns.count;
+    size_t rightBegin = columns.count;
+    if (pivotBegin >= columns.begin && pivotBegin < columns.begin + columns.count) {
+        leftEnd = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+        rightBegin = leftEnd + blockSize;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+        if (globalRow >= pivotBegin && globalRow < pivotBegin + blockSize) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        unsigned int* const row = dist.data() + i * columns.count;
+        const unsigned int* const column = pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize;
+        const size_t rangeBegins[2] = {0, rightBegin};
+        const size_t rangeEnds[2] = {leftEnd, columns.count};
+
+        for (int range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+            for (size_t tileBegin = rangeBegins[range]; tileBegin < rangeEnds[range];
+                 tileBegin += COLUMN_TILE) {
+                const size_t tileEnd = std::min(tileBegin + COLUMN_TILE, rangeEnds[range]);
+                for (size_t q = 0; q < blockSize; ++q) {
+                    const unsigned int* const qRow = pivotRows.data() + q * columns.count;
+                    updateContiguous(row, qRow, column[q], tileBegin, tileEnd);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const Partition rows,
+                   const Partition columns,
+                   const int processRow,
+                   const int processColumn,
+                   const int processRows,
+                   const int processColumns,
+                   MPI_Comm rowCommunicator,
+                   MPI_Comm columnCommunicator) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivot;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns;
+    pivot.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * BLOCK_SIZE);
+    pivotRows.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * columns.count);
+    pivotColumns.reserve(rows.count * BLOCK_SIZE);
+
+    for (size_t pivotBegin = 0; pivotBegin < numNodes;) {
+        const int pivotProcessRow = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processRows);
+        const int pivotProcessColumn = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processColumns);
+        const Partition pivotRowPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, pivotProcessRow);
+        const Partition pivotColumnPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
+                                                             pivotProcessColumn);
+        const size_t blockEnd = std::min({pivotBegin + BLOCK_SIZE,
+                                          pivotRowPartition.begin + pivotRowPartition.count,
+                                          pivotColumnPartition.begin + pivotColumnPartition.count,
+                                          numNodes});
+        const size_t blockSize = blockEnd - pivotBegin;
+        pivot.resize(blockSize * blockSize);
+
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow && processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+                const unsigned int* const source = dist.data()
+                                                 + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
+                                                 + localPivotColumn;
+                std::copy_n(source, blockSize, pivot.data() + i * blockSize);
+            }
+            closePivot(pivot, blockSize);
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+                unsigned int* const destination = dist.data()
+                                                + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
+                                                + localPivotColumn;
+                std::copy_n(pivot.data() + i * blockSize, blockSize, destination);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const int pivotCount = static_cast<int>(blockSize * blockSize);
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
+            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn,
+                      rowCommunicator);
+            updatePivotRows(dist, pivot, columns, pivotBegin - rows.begin,
+                            pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        }
+        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessRow,
+                      columnCommunicator);
+            updatePivotColumns(dist, pivot, rows, columns, pivotBegin - columns.begin,
+                               pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        }
+
+        pivotRows.resize(blockSize * columns.count);
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
+            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
+            if (columns.count != 0) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + localPivotRow * columns.count,
+                            blockSize * columns.count, pivotRows.data());
+            }
+        }
+
+        pivotColumns.resize(rows.count * blockSize);
+        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count + localPivotColumn,
+                            blockSize, pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const size_t pivotRowCount = blockSize * columns.count;
+        const size_t pivotColumnCount = rows.count * blockSize;
+        if (pivotRowCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)
+            || pivotColumnCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI panel exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        MPI_Ibcast(pivotRows.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRowCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   pivotProcessRow, columnCommunicator, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumns.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumnCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   pivotProcessColumn, rowCommunicator, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        updateRemaining(dist, pivotRows, pivotColumns, rows, columns,
+                        pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        pivotBegin = blockEnd;
+    }
+}
+
+void reduceUnsignedSum(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                       std::vector<unsigned int>& result,
+                       const int root,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < local.size()) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(local.size() - offset,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Reduce(local.data() + offset, result.data() + offset, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   MPI_SUM, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<size_t>(count);
+    }
+}

+bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+                    const Partition rows,
+                    const Partition columns,

+                    MPI_Comm communicator) {

+                                            + (sampleSize + i) * numNodes + rows.begin;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < rows.count; ++k) {
+                destination[k] = dist[k * columns.count + localColumn];
+            }
         }
     }

-                               i, j, k);
-                        return false;
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> samples(rank == 0 ? localSamples.size() : 0);
+    // MPI requires a valid receive pointer even on some older implementations.
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        samples.resize(localSamples.size());
+    }
+    reduceUnsignedSum(localSamples, samples, 0, communicator);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (globalBadDiagonal != numNodes) {
+            std::printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%llu,%llu] is not zero\n",
+                        globalBadDiagonal, globalBadDiagonal);
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; valid != 0 && i < sampleSize; ++i) {

+void copyLocalToSlab(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& slab,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const size_t columnBegin,
+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + i * columns, columns,
+                    slab.data() + i * numNodes + columnBegin);
+    }

+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                             const size_t numNodes,
+                             const Partition rows,
+                             const Partition columns,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int processRows,
+                             const int processColumns,
+                             MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr int RESULT_TAG = 4107;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (rows.count != 0 && columns.count != 0) {
+            const size_t rowsPerMessage = std::max(
+                static_cast<size_t>(1), static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / columns.count);
+            for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < rows.count; rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+                const size_t messageRows = std::min(rowsPerMessage, rows.count - rowOffset);
+                const int count = static_cast<int>(messageRows * columns.count);
+                MPI_Send(dist.data() + rowOffset * columns.count, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                         0, RESULT_TAG, communicator);
+            }
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    std::printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
+    std::printf("Name: DistanceMatrix\n");
+    const size_t elements = numNodes * numNodes;
+    std::printf("Elements: %zu\n", elements);
+    if (elements == 0) {
+        std::printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
+        return;
+    }
+
+    hash resultHash;
+    unsigned int minimum = std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max();
+    unsigned int maximum = 0;
+    const size_t sampleIndices[5] = {0, elements / 4, elements / 2,
+                                     3 * elements / 4, elements - 1};
+    unsigned int sampleValues[5] = {};
+
+    for (int processRow = 0; processRow < processRows; ++processRow) {
+        const Partition slabRows = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, processRow);
+        if (slabRows.count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        std::vector<unsigned int> slab(slabRows.count * numNodes);
+
+        for (int processColumn = 0; processColumn < processColumns; ++processColumn) {
+            const Partition slabColumns = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
+                                                        processColumn);
+            if (slabColumns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int sourceRank = processRow * processColumns + processColumn;
+            if (sourceRank == 0) {
+                copyLocalToSlab(dist, slab, slabRows.count, slabColumns.count,
+                                slabColumns.begin, numNodes);
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rowsPerMessage = std::max(
+                static_cast<size_t>(1), static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / slabColumns.count);
+            for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < slabRows.count;
+                 rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+                const size_t messageRows = std::min(rowsPerMessage,
+                                                    slabRows.count - rowOffset);
+                MPI_Datatype slabType;
+                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(messageRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(slabColumns.count),
+                                static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, &slabType);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&slabType);
+                MPI_Recv(slab.data() + rowOffset * numNodes + slabColumns.begin,
+                         1, slabType, sourceRank, RESULT_TAG, communicator,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&slabType);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const size_t globalOffset = slabRows.begin * numNodes;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < slab.size(); ++i) {
+            const unsigned int value = slab[i];
+            resultHash.add(value);
+            minimum = std::min(minimum, value);
+            maximum = std::max(maximum, value);
+        }
+        for (int sample = 0; sample < 5; ++sample) {
+            if (sampleIndices[sample] >= globalOffset
+                && sampleIndices[sample] < globalOffset + slab.size()) {
+                sampleValues[sample] = slab[sampleIndices[sample] - globalOffset];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(minimum));
+    std::printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(maximum));
+    for (int sample = 0; sample < 5; ++sample) {
+        std::printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", sampleIndices[sample],
+                    static_cast<long long>(sampleValues[sample]));
+    }
+    std::printf("Hash: %016llx\n",
+                static_cast<unsigned long long>(resultHash.get()));
+    std::printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
+}
+

Builds are warning-free, and singleton execution works without `mpirun` as well.

    15	constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
    16	constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
    17	constexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 64;
    18	
    19	struct Partition {
    20	    size_t begin;
    21	    size_t count;
    22	};
    23	
    24	Partition partitionFor(const size_t n, const int parts, const int coordinate) noexcept {
    25	    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    26	    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    27	    const size_t extra = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1 : 0;
    28	    const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base
    29	                       + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
    30	    return {begin, base + extra};
    31	}
    32	
    33	int ownerOf(const size_t index, const size_t n, const int parts) noexcept {
    34	    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    35	    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    36	    const size_t largeRegion = (base + 1) * remainder;
    37	
    38	    if (index < largeRegion) {
    39	        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1));
    40	    }
    41	    return static_cast<int>(remainder + (index - largeRegion) / base);
    42	}
    43	
    44	// rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping the
    45	// state lets every rank recreate its rectangular portion of the original
    46	// serial random stream without storing or communicating the complete matrix.
    47	unsigned int advanceRandSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed, size_t calls) noexcept {
    48	    static_assert(std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::digits == 32);
    49	    constexpr uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
    50	    constexpr uint32_t increment = 12345u;
    51	
    52	    uint32_t callMultiplier = 1;
    53	    uint32_t callIncrement = 0;
    54	    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
    55	        callIncrement = multiplier * callIncrement + increment;
    56	        callMultiplier = multiplier * callMultiplier;
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1;
    60	    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0;
    61	    while (calls != 0) {
    62	        if ((calls & 1U) != 0) {
    63	            accumulatedIncrement = callMultiplier * accumulatedIncrement + callIncrement;
    64	            accumulatedMultiplier = callMultiplier * accumulatedMultiplier;
    65	        }
    66	        callIncrement = (callMultiplier + 1U) * callIncrement;
    67	        callMultiplier *= callMultiplier;
    68	        calls >>= 1U;
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    return accumulatedMultiplier * initialSeed + accumulatedIncrement;
    72	}
    73	
    74	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
    75	                              const size_t numNodes,
    76	                              const Partition rows,
    77	                              const Partition columns,
    78	                              const unsigned int rangeMin,
    79	                              const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    80	    if (columns.count == 0) {
    81	        return;
    82	    }
    83	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    84	
    85	    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rows.count; ++localRow) {
    86	        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + localRow;
    87	        unsigned int seed = advanceRandSeed(42U, globalRow * numNodes + columns.begin);
    88	        unsigned int* const row = dist.data() + localRow * columns.count;
    89	
    90	        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < columns.count; ++localColumn) {
    91	            row[localColumn] = rangeMin + static_cast<unsigned int>(
    92	                range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
    93	        }
    94	
    95	        if (globalRow >= columns.begin && globalRow < columns.begin + columns.count) {
    96	            row[globalRow - columns.begin] = 0;
    97	        }
    98	    }
    99	}
   100	
   101	inline void updateContiguous(unsigned int* __restrict destination,
   102	                             const unsigned int* __restrict source,
   103	                             const unsigned int leftDistance,
   104	                             const size_t begin,
   105	                             const size_t end) noexcept {
   106	    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
   107	        const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + source[j];
   108	        if (candidate < destination[j]) {
   109	            destination[j] = candidate;
   110	        }
   228	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   229	                   const size_t numNodes,
   230	                   const Partition rows,
   231	                   const Partition columns,
   232	                   const int processRow,
   233	                   const int processColumn,
   234	                   const int processRows,
   235	                   const int processColumns,
   236	                   MPI_Comm rowCommunicator,
   237	                   MPI_Comm columnCommunicator) {
   238	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivot;
   239	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows;
   240	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns;
   241	    pivot.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * BLOCK_SIZE);
   242	    pivotRows.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * columns.count);
   243	    pivotColumns.reserve(rows.count * BLOCK_SIZE);
   244	
   245	    for (size_t pivotBegin = 0; pivotBegin < numNodes;) {
   246	        const int pivotProcessRow = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processRows);
   247	        const int pivotProcessColumn = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processColumns);
   248	        const Partition pivotRowPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, pivotProcessRow);
   249	        const Partition pivotColumnPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
   250	                                                             pivotProcessColumn);
   251	        const size_t blockEnd = std::min({pivotBegin + BLOCK_SIZE,
   252	                                          pivotRowPartition.begin + pivotRowPartition.count,
   253	                                          pivotColumnPartition.begin + pivotColumnPartition.count,
   254	                                          numNodes});
   255	        const size_t blockSize = blockEnd - pivotBegin;
   256	        pivot.resize(blockSize * blockSize);
   257	
   258	        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow && processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
   259	            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
   260	            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
   261	            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
   262	                const unsigned int* const source = dist.data()
   263	                                                 + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
   264	                                                 + localPivotColumn;
   265	                std::copy_n(source, blockSize, pivot.data() + i * blockSize);
   266	            }
   267	            closePivot(pivot, blockSize);
   268	            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
   269	                unsigned int* const destination = dist.data()
   270	                                                + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
   271	                                                + localPivotColumn;
   272	                std::copy_n(pivot.data() + i * blockSize, blockSize, destination);
   273	            }
   274	        }
   275	
   276	        const int pivotCount = static_cast<int>(blockSize * blockSize);
   277	        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
   278	            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn,
   279	                      rowCommunicator);
   280	            updatePivotRows(dist, pivot, columns, pivotBegin - rows.begin,
   281	                            pivotBegin, blockSize);
   282	        }
   283	        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
   284	            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessRow,
   285	                      columnCommunicator);
   286	            updatePivotColumns(dist, pivot, rows, columns, pivotBegin - columns.begin,
   287	                               pivotBegin, blockSize);
   288	        }
   289	
   290	        pivotRows.resize(blockSize * columns.count);
   291	        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
   292	            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
   293	            if (columns.count != 0) {
   294	                std::copy_n(dist.data() + localPivotRow * columns.count,
   295	                            blockSize * columns.count, pivotRows.data());
   296	            }
   297	        }
   298	
   299	        pivotColumns.resize(rows.count * blockSize);
   300	        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
   301	            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
   302	            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
   303	                std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count + localPivotColumn,
   304	                            blockSize, pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize);
   305	            }
   306	        }
   307	
   308	        const size_t pivotRowCount = blockSize * columns.count;
   309	        const size_t pivotColumnCount = rows.count * blockSize;
   310	        if (pivotRowCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)
   311	            || pivotColumnCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   312	            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI panel exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
   313	            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   314	        }
   315	
   316	        MPI_Request requests[2];
   317	        MPI_Ibcast(pivotRows.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRowCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   318	                   pivotProcessRow, columnCommunicator, &requests[0]);
   319	        MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumns.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumnCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   320	                   pivotProcessColumn, rowCommunicator, &requests[1]);
   321	        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   322	
   323	        updateRemaining(dist, pivotRows, pivotColumns, rows, columns,
   324	                        pivotBegin, blockSize);
   325	        pivotBegin = blockEnd;
   326	    }
   327	}
   328	
   329	void reduceUnsignedSum(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
   330	                       std::vector<unsigned int>& result,
   331	                       const int root,
   332	                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
   333	    size_t offset = 0;
   334	    while (offset < local.size()) {
   335	        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(local.size() - offset,
   336	                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
   337	        MPI_Reduce(local.data() + offset, result.data() + offset, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
   338	                   MPI_SUM, root, communicator);
   339	        offset += static_cast<size_t>(count);
   340	    }
   343	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   344	                    const size_t numNodes,
   345	                    const Partition rows,
   346	                    const Partition columns,
   347	                    const int rank,
   348	                    MPI_Comm communicator) {
   349	    unsigned long long firstBadDiagonal = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numNodes);
   350	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
   351	        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
   352	        if (globalRow >= columns.begin && globalRow < columns.begin + columns.count
   353	            && dist[i * columns.count + globalRow - columns.begin] != 0) {
   354	            firstBadDiagonal = std::min(firstBadDiagonal,
   355	                                        static_cast<unsigned long long>(globalRow));
   356	        }
   357	    }
   358	
   359	    unsigned long long globalBadDiagonal = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numNodes);
   360	    MPI_Reduce(&firstBadDiagonal, &globalBadDiagonal, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   361	               MPI_MIN, 0, communicator);
   362	
   363	    const size_t sampleSize = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));
   364	    std::vector<unsigned int> localSamples(2 * sampleSize * numNodes, 0);
   365	    for (size_t i = 0; i < sampleSize; ++i) {
   366	        if (columns.count != 0 && i >= rows.begin && i < rows.begin + rows.count) {
   367	            const unsigned int* const source = dist.data() + (i - rows.begin) * columns.count;
   368	            std::copy_n(source, columns.count,
   369	                        localSamples.data() + i * numNodes + columns.begin);
   370	        }
   371	        if (i >= columns.begin && i < columns.begin + columns.count) {
   372	            const size_t localColumn = i - columns.begin;
   373	            unsigned int* const destination = localSamples.data()
   374	                                            + (sampleSize + i) * numNodes + rows.begin;
   375	            for (size_t k = 0; k < rows.count; ++k) {
   376	                destination[k] = dist[k * columns.count + localColumn];
   377	            }
   378	        }
   379	    }
   380	
   381	    std::vector<unsigned int> samples(rank == 0 ? localSamples.size() : 0);
   382	    // MPI requires a valid receive pointer even on some older implementations.
   383	    if (rank != 0) {
   384	        samples.resize(localSamples.size());
   385	    }
   386	    reduceUnsignedSum(localSamples, samples, 0, communicator);
   387	
   388	    int valid = 1;
   389	    if (rank == 0) {
   390	        if (globalBadDiagonal != numNodes) {
   391	            std::printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%llu,%llu] is not zero\n",
   392	                        globalBadDiagonal, globalBadDiagonal);
   393	            valid = 0;
   394	        }
   395	
   396	        for (size_t i = 0; valid != 0 && i < sampleSize; ++i) {
   397	            const unsigned int* const sourceRow = samples.data() + i * numNodes;
   398	            for (size_t j = 0; valid != 0 && j < sampleSize; ++j) {
   399	                const unsigned int distIJ = sourceRow[j];
   400	                const unsigned int* const destinationColumn = samples.data()
   401	                                                                + (sampleSize + j) * numNodes;
   402	                for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   403	                    const unsigned int distIK = sourceRow[k];
   404	                    const unsigned int distKJ = destinationColumn[k];
   405	                    if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF && distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
   406	                        std::printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at "
   407	                                    "[%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", i, j, k);
   408	                        valid = 0;
   409	                        break;
   410	                    }
   411	                }
   412	            }
   413	        }
   414	    }
   415	
   416	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
   417	    return valid != 0;
   418	}
   419	
   420	void copyLocalToSlab(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
   421	                     std::vector<unsigned int>& slab,
   422	                     const size_t rows,
   423	                     const size_t columns,
   424	                     const size_t columnBegin,
   425	                     const size_t numNodes) {
   426	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
   427	        std::copy_n(local.data() + i * columns, columns,
   428	                    slab.data() + i * numNodes + columnBegin);
   429	    }
   430	}
   431	
   432	void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   433	                             const size_t numNodes,
   434	                             const Partition rows,
   435	                             const Partition columns,
   436	                             const int rank,
   437	                             const int processRows,
   438	                             const int processColumns,
   439	                             MPI_Comm communicator) {
   440	    constexpr int RESULT_TAG = 4107;
   441	
   442	    if (rank != 0) {
   443	        if (rows.count != 0 && columns.count != 0) {
   444	            const size_t rowsPerMessage = std::max(
   445	                static_cast<size_t>(1), static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / columns.count);
   446	            for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < rows.count; rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
   447	                const size_t messageRows = std::min(rowsPerMessage, rows.count - rowOffset);
   448	                const int count = static_cast<int>(messageRows * columns.count);
   449	                MPI_Send(dist.data() + rowOffset * columns.count, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
   450	                         0, RESULT_TAG, communicator);
   451	            }
   452	        }
   453	        return;
   454	    }
   455	
   456	    std::printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
   457	    std::printf("Name: DistanceMatrix\n");
   458	    const size_t elements = numNodes * numNodes;
   459	    std::printf("Elements: %zu\n", elements);
   460	    if (elements == 0) {
   461	        std::printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
   462	        return;
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    hash resultHash;
   466	    unsigned int minimum = std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max();
   467	    unsigned int maximum = 0;
   468	    const size_t sampleIndices[5] = {0, elements / 4, elements / 2,
   469	                                     3 * elements / 4, elements - 1};
   470	    unsigned int sampleValues[5] = {};
   471	
   472	    for (int processRow = 0; processRow < processRows; ++processRow) {
   473	        const Partition slabRows = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, processRow);
   474	        if (slabRows.count == 0) {
   475	            continue;
   476	        }
   477	        std::vector<unsigned int> slab(slabRows.count * numNodes);
   478	
   479	        for (int processColumn = 0; processColumn < processColumns; ++processColumn) {
   480	            const Partition slabColumns = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
   481	                                                        processColumn);
   482	            if (slabColumns.count == 0) {
   483	                continue;
   484	            }
   485	            const int sourceRank = processRow * processColumns + processColumn;
   486	            if (sourceRank == 0) {
   487	                copyLocalToSlab(dist, slab, slabRows.count, slabColumns.count,
   488	                                slabColumns.begin, numNodes);
   489	                continue;
   490	            }
   491	
   492	            const size_t rowsPerMessage = std::max(
   493	                static_cast<size_t>(1), static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / slabColumns.count);
   494	            for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < slabRows.count;
   495	                 rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
   496	                const size_t messageRows = std::min(rowsPerMessage,
   497	                                                    slabRows.count - rowOffset);
   498	                MPI_Datatype slabType;
   499	                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(messageRows),
   500	                                static_cast<int>(slabColumns.count),
   501	                                static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, &slabType);
   502	                MPI_Type_commit(&slabType);
   503	                MPI_Recv(slab.data() + rowOffset * numNodes + slabColumns.begin,
   504	                         1, slabType, sourceRank, RESULT_TAG, communicator,
   505	                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   506	                MPI_Type_free(&slabType);
   507	            }
   508	        }
   509	
   510	        const size_t globalOffset = slabRows.begin * numNodes;
   511	        for (size_t i = 0; i < slab.size(); ++i) {
   512	            const unsigned int value = slab[i];
   513	            resultHash.add(value);
   514	            minimum = std::min(minimum, value);
   515	            maximum = std::max(maximum, value);
   516	        }
   517	        for (int sample = 0; sample < 5; ++sample) {
   518	            if (sampleIndices[sample] >= globalOffset
   519	                && sampleIndices[sample] < globalOffset + slab.size()) {
   520	                sampleValues[sample] = slab[sampleIndices[sample] - globalOffset];
   521	            }
   522	        }
   523	    }
   524	
   525	    std::printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(minimum));
   526	    std::printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(maximum));
   527	    for (int sample = 0; sample < 5; ++sample) {
   528	        std::printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", sampleIndices[sample],
   529	                    static_cast<long long>(sampleValues[sample]));
   530	    }
   531	    std::printf("Hash: %016llx\n",
   532	                static_cast<unsigned long long>(resultHash.get()));
   533	    std::printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
   534	}

   545	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   545	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   546	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   547	
   548	    int rank = 0;
   549	    int worldSize = 1;
   550	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   551	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   552	
   553	    size_t numNodes = 512;
   554	    bool validate = false;
   555	    bool printResults = false;
   556	    int parseStatus = 0;
   557	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   558	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   559	            numNodes = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   560	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   561	            validate = true;
   562	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   563	            printResults = true;
   564	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   565	            parseStatus = 2;
   566	        } else {
   567	            if (rank == 0) {
   568	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   569	            }
   570	            parseStatus = 1;
   571	        }
   572	    }
   573	
   574	    if (parseStatus != 0) {
   575	        if (rank == 0) {
   576	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   577	        }
   578	        MPI_Finalize();
   579	        return parseStatus == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   580	    }
   581	
   582	    if (numNodes > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)
   583	        || (numNodes != 0 && numNodes > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / numNodes)) {
   584	        if (rank == 0) {
   585	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Number of nodes is too large for this MPI implementation\n");
   586	        }
   587	        MPI_Finalize();
   588	        return 1;
   589	    }
   590	
   591	    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
   592	    MPI_Dims_create(worldSize, 2, dimensions);
   593	    const int processRow = rank / dimensions[1];
   594	    const int processColumn = rank % dimensions[1];
   595	    const Partition rows = partitionFor(numNodes, dimensions[0], processRow);
   596	    const Partition columns = partitionFor(numNodes, dimensions[1], processColumn);
   597	
   598	    MPI_Comm rowCommunicator;
   599	    MPI_Comm columnCommunicator;
   600	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, processRow, processColumn, &rowCommunicator);
   601	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, processColumn, processRow, &columnCommunicator);
   602	
   603	    if (rank == 0) {
   604	        std::printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
   605	        std::printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
   606	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   607	        std::printf("Initializing graph...\n");
   608	    }
   609	
   610	    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(rows.count * columns.count);
   611	    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, rows, columns, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
   612	
   613	    if (rank == 0) {
   614	        std::printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
   615	    }
   616	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   617	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   618	    floydWarshall(dist, numNodes, rows, columns, processRow, processColumn,
   619	                  dimensions[0], dimensions[1], rowCommunicator, columnCommunicator);
   620	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   621	
   622	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   623	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   624	    if (rank == 0) {
   625	        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
   626	        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
   627	        const double operations = static_cast<double>(numNodes)
   628	                                * static_cast<double>(numNodes)
   629	                                * static_cast<double>(numNodes);
   630	        const double gops = operations / elapsed / 1e9;
   631	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gops);
   632	    }
   633	
   634	    if (printResults) {
   635	        printDistributedResults(dist, numNodes, rows, columns, rank,
   636	                                dimensions[0], dimensions[1], MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   637	    }
   638	
   639	    int exitStatus = 0;
   640	    if (validate) {
   641	        if (rank == 0) {
   642	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   643	        }
   644	        const bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes, rows, columns,
   645	                                          rank, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   646	        if (rank == 0) {
   647	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   648	        }
   649	        exitStatus = valid ? 0 : 1;
   650	    }
   651	
   652	    MPI_Comm_free(&rowCommunicator);
   653	    MPI_Comm_free(&columnCommunicator);
   654	    MPI_Finalize();
   655	    return exitStatus;
   656	}

+struct Partition {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Partition partitionFor(const size_t n, const int parts, const int coordinate) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t extra = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base
+                       + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
+    return {begin, base + extra};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t index, const size_t n, const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t largeRegion = (base + 1) * remainder;
+
+    if (index < largeRegion) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(remainder + (index - largeRegion) / base);
+}
+
+// rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping the
+// state lets every rank recreate its rectangular portion of the original
+// serial random stream without storing or communicating the complete matrix.
+unsigned int advanceRandSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed, size_t calls) noexcept {
+    static_assert(std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::digits == 32);
+    constexpr uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    constexpr uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+
+    uint32_t callMultiplier = 1;
+    uint32_t callIncrement = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
+        callIncrement = multiplier * callIncrement + increment;
+        callMultiplier = multiplier * callMultiplier;
+    }
 
-// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
-inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
-    return j * n + i;
+    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1;
+    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0;
+    while (calls != 0) {
+        if ((calls & 1U) != 0) {
+            accumulatedIncrement = callMultiplier * accumulatedIncrement + callIncrement;
+            accumulatedMultiplier = callMultiplier * accumulatedMultiplier;
+        }
+        callIncrement = (callMultiplier + 1U) * callIncrement;
+        callMultiplier *= callMultiplier;
+        calls >>= 1U;
+    }
+
+    return accumulatedMultiplier * initialSeed + accumulatedIncrement;

+void updatePivotColumns(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+void updateRemaining(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRows,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumns,
+                     const Partition rows,
+                     const Partition columns,
+                     const size_t pivotBegin,
+                     const size_t blockSize) noexcept {
+    if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    constexpr size_t COLUMN_TILE = 256;
+    size_t leftEnd = columns.count;
+    size_t rightBegin = columns.count;
+    if (pivotBegin >= columns.begin && pivotBegin < columns.begin + columns.count) {
+        leftEnd = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+        rightBegin = leftEnd + blockSize;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+        if (globalRow >= pivotBegin && globalRow < pivotBegin + blockSize) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        unsigned int* const row = dist.data() + i * columns.count;
+        const unsigned int* const column = pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize;
+        const size_t rangeBegins[2] = {0, rightBegin};
+        const size_t rangeEnds[2] = {leftEnd, columns.count};
+
+        for (int range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+            for (size_t tileBegin = rangeBegins[range]; tileBegin < rangeEnds[range];
+                 tileBegin += COLUMN_TILE) {
+                const size_t tileEnd = std::min(tileBegin + COLUMN_TILE, rangeEnds[range]);
+                for (size_t q = 0; q < blockSize; ++q) {
+                    const unsigned int* const qRow = pivotRows.data() + q * columns.count;
+                    updateContiguous(row, qRow, column[q], tileBegin, tileEnd);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const Partition rows,
+                   const Partition columns,
+                   const int processRow,
+                   const int processColumn,
+                   const int processRows,
+                   const int processColumns,
+                   MPI_Comm rowCommunicator,
+                   MPI_Comm columnCommunicator) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivot;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns;
+    pivot.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * BLOCK_SIZE);
+    pivotRows.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * columns.count);
+    pivotColumns.reserve(rows.count * BLOCK_SIZE);
+
+    for (size_t pivotBegin = 0; pivotBegin < numNodes;) {
+        const int pivotProcessRow = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processRows);
+        const int pivotProcessColumn = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processColumns);
+        const Partition pivotRowPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, pivotProcessRow);
+        const Partition pivotColumnPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
+                                                             pivotProcessColumn);
+        const size_t blockEnd = std::min({pivotBegin + BLOCK_SIZE,
+                                          pivotRowPartition.begin + pivotRowPartition.count,
+                                          pivotColumnPartition.begin + pivotColumnPartition.count,
+                                          numNodes});
+        const size_t blockSize = blockEnd - pivotBegin;
+        pivot.resize(blockSize * blockSize);
+
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow && processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+                const unsigned int* const source = dist.data()
+                                                 + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
+                                                 + localPivotColumn;
+                std::copy_n(source, blockSize, pivot.data() + i * blockSize);
+            }
+            closePivot(pivot, blockSize);
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+                unsigned int* const destination = dist.data()
+                                                + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
+                                                + localPivotColumn;
+                std::copy_n(pivot.data() + i * blockSize, blockSize, destination);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const int pivotCount = static_cast<int>(blockSize * blockSize);
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
+            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn,
+                      rowCommunicator);
+            updatePivotRows(dist, pivot, columns, pivotBegin - rows.begin,
+                            pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        }
+        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessRow,
+                      columnCommunicator);
+            updatePivotColumns(dist, pivot, rows, columns, pivotBegin - columns.begin,
+                               pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        }
+
+        pivotRows.resize(blockSize * columns.count);
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
+            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
+            if (columns.count != 0) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + localPivotRow * columns.count,
+                            blockSize * columns.count, pivotRows.data());
+            }
+        }
+
+        pivotColumns.resize(rows.count * blockSize);
+        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count + localPivotColumn,
+                            blockSize, pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const size_t pivotRowCount = blockSize * columns.count;
+        const size_t pivotColumnCount = rows.count * blockSize;
+        if (pivotRowCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)
+            || pivotColumnCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI panel exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        MPI_Ibcast(pivotRows.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRowCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   pivotProcessRow, columnCommunicator, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumns.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumnCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   pivotProcessColumn, rowCommunicator, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        updateRemaining(dist, pivotRows, pivotColumns, rows, columns,
+                        pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        pivotBegin = blockEnd;
+    }
+}
+
+void reduceUnsignedSum(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                       std::vector<unsigned int>& result,
+                       const int root,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < local.size()) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(local.size() - offset,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Reduce(local.data() + offset, result.data() + offset, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   MPI_SUM, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<size_t>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                    const size_t numNodes,
+                    const Partition rows,
+                    const Partition columns,
+                    const int rank,
+                    MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    unsigned long long firstBadDiagonal = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numNodes);
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+        if (globalRow >= columns.begin && globalRow < columns.begin + columns.count
+            && dist[i * columns.count + globalRow - columns.begin] != 0) {
+            firstBadDiagonal = std::min(firstBadDiagonal,
+                                        static_cast<unsigned long long>(globalRow));
+        }
+    }
+
+    unsigned long long globalBadDiagonal = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numNodes);
+    MPI_Reduce(&firstBadDiagonal, &globalBadDiagonal, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
+               MPI_MIN, 0, communicator);
+
+    const size_t sampleSize = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));
+    std::vector<unsigned int> localSamples(2 * sampleSize * numNodes, 0);
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < sampleSize; ++i) {
+        if (columns.count != 0 && i >= rows.begin && i < rows.begin + rows.count) {
+            const unsigned int* const source = dist.data() + (i - rows.begin) * columns.count;
+            std::copy_n(source, columns.count,
+                        localSamples.data() + i * numNodes + columns.begin);
+        }
+        if (i >= columns.begin && i < columns.begin + columns.count) {
+            const size_t localColumn = i - columns.begin;
+            unsigned int* const destination = localSamples.data()
+                                            + (sampleSize + i) * numNodes + rows.begin;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < rows.count; ++k) {
+                destination[k] = dist[k * columns.count + localColumn];
+            }
         }
     }

+void copyLocalToSlab(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& slab,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const size_t columnBegin,
+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + i * columns, columns,
+                    slab.data() + i * numNodes + columnBegin);
+    }
 }
 
+void printDistributedResults(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                             const size_t numNodes,
+                             const Partition rows,
+                             const Partition columns,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int processRows,
+                             const int processColumns,
+                             MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr int RESULT_TAG = 4107;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (rows.count != 0 && columns.count != 0) {
+            const size_t rowsPerMessage = std::max(
+                static_cast<size_t>(1), static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / columns.count);
+            for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < rows.count; rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+                const size_t messageRows = std::min(rowsPerMessage, rows.count - rowOffset);
+                const int count = static_cast<int>(messageRows * columns.count);
+                MPI_Send(dist.data() + rowOffset * columns.count, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                         0, RESULT_TAG, communicator);
+            }
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    std::printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
+    std::printf("Name: DistanceMatrix\n");
+    const size_t elements = numNodes * numNodes;
+    std::printf("Elements: %zu\n", elements);
+    if (elements == 0) {
+        std::printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
+        return;
+    }
+
+    hash resultHash;
+    unsigned int minimum = std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max();
+    unsigned int maximum = 0;
+    const size_t sampleIndices[5] = {0, elements / 4, elements / 2,
+                                     3 * elements / 4, elements - 1};
+    unsigned int sampleValues[5] = {};
+
+    for (int processRow = 0; processRow < processRows; ++processRow) {
+        const Partition slabRows = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, processRow);
+        if (slabRows.count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        std::vector<unsigned int> slab(slabRows.count * numNodes);
+
+        for (int processColumn = 0; processColumn < processColumns; ++processColumn) {
+            const Partition slabColumns = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
+                                                        processColumn);
+            if (slabColumns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int sourceRank = processRow * processColumns + processColumn;
+            if (sourceRank == 0) {
+                copyLocalToSlab(dist, slab, slabRows.count, slabColumns.count,
+                                slabColumns.begin, numNodes);
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rowsPerMessage = std::max(
+                static_cast<size_t>(1), static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / slabColumns.count);
+            for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < slabRows.count;
+                 rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+                const size_t messageRows = std::min(rowsPerMessage,
+                                                    slabRows.count - rowOffset);
+                MPI_Datatype slabType;
+                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(messageRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(slabColumns.count),
+                                static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, &slabType);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&slabType);
+                MPI_Recv(slab.data() + rowOffset * numNodes + slabColumns.begin,
+                         1, slabType, sourceRank, RESULT_TAG, communicator,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&slabType);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const size_t globalOffset = slabRows.begin * numNodes;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < slab.size(); ++i) {
+            const unsigned int value = slab[i];
+            resultHash.add(value);
+            minimum = std::min(minimum, value);
+            maximum = std::max(maximum, value);
+        }
+        for (int sample = 0; sample < 5; ++sample) {
+            if (sampleIndices[sample] >= globalOffset
+                && sampleIndices[sample] < globalOffset + slab.size()) {
+                sampleValues[sample] = slab[sampleIndices[sample] - globalOffset];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    std::printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(minimum));
+    std::printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(maximum));
+    for (int sample = 0; sample < 5; ++sample) {
+        std::printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", sampleIndices[sample],
+                    static_cast<long long>(sampleValues[sample]));
+    }
+    std::printf("Hash: %016llx\n",
+                static_cast<unsigned long long>(resultHash.get()));
+    std::printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
+}
+

codex
Implemented mandatory MPI parallelization while preserving the `floydwarshall` executable name.

Key changes:

- 2D process-grid matrix distribution with approximately `O(N²/P)` memory per rank.
- Cache-blocked Floyd–Warshall with nonblocking MPI panel broadcasts.
- Deterministic distributed initialization matching the original serial random stream.
- Distributed validation and result reconstruction with root-only output.
- MPI-aware synchronized timing using the slowest rank.
- Mandatory MPI discovery/linking in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/floydwarshall_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/floydwarshall/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Implementation in [floydwarshall.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/floydwarshall_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp:228).

Verification:

- Warning-free release build.
- Exact serial-reference hashes across block boundaries and 1, 2, 3, 4, and 6 MPI ranks.
- Validation and UBSan checks passed, including empty partitions.
- At `n=512`, measured 2.64, 4.64, and 8.30 GOPS on 1, 2, and 4 local ranks respectively.

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 64;
+
+struct Partition {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Partition partitionFor(const size_t n, const int parts, const int coordinate) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t extra = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base
+                       + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
+    return {begin, base + extra};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t index, const size_t n, const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t largeRegion = (base + 1) * remainder;
+
+    if (index < largeRegion) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(remainder + (index - largeRegion) / base);
+}
+
+// rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping the
+// state lets every rank recreate its rectangular portion of the original
+// serial random stream without storing or communicating the complete matrix.
+unsigned int advanceRandSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed, size_t calls) noexcept {
+    static_assert(std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::digits == 32);
+    constexpr uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    constexpr uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+
+    uint32_t callMultiplier = 1;
+    uint32_t callIncrement = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
+        callIncrement = multiplier * callIncrement + increment;
+        callMultiplier = multiplier * callMultiplier;
+    }
 
-// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
-inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
-    return j * n + i;
+    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1;
+    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0;
+    while (calls != 0) {
+        if ((calls & 1U) != 0) {
+            accumulatedIncrement = callMultiplier * accumulatedIncrement + callIncrement;
+            accumulatedMultiplier = callMultiplier * accumulatedMultiplier;
+        }
+        callIncrement = (callMultiplier + 1U) * callIncrement;
+        callMultiplier *= callMultiplier;
+        calls >>= 1U;
+    }
+
+    return accumulatedMultiplier * initialSeed + accumulatedIncrement;
 }

+inline void updateContiguous(unsigned int* __restrict destination,
+                             const unsigned int* __restrict source,
+                             const unsigned int leftDistance,
+                             const size_t begin,
+                             const size_t end) noexcept {

+        if (candidate < destination[j]) {

+void updatePivotRows(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivot,
+                     const Partition columns,
+                     const size_t localPivotRow,
+                     const size_t pivotBegin,

+    if (columns.count == 0) {

+            if (i == q) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            unsigned int* const row = dist.data()
+                                    + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count;
+            const unsigned int leftDistance = pivot[i * blockSize + q];
+            updateContiguous(row, qRow, leftDistance, 0, leftEnd);
+            updateContiguous(row, qRow, leftDistance, rightBegin, columns.count);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updatePivotColumns(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivot,
+                        const Partition rows,
+                        const Partition columns,
+                        const size_t localPivotColumn,
+                        const size_t pivotBegin,
+                        const size_t blockSize) noexcept {
+    for (size_t q = 0; q < blockSize; ++q) {
+        const unsigned int* const pivotRow = pivot.data() + q * blockSize;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+            if (globalRow >= pivotBegin && globalRow < pivotBegin + blockSize) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            unsigned int* const row = dist.data() + i * columns.count + localPivotColumn;
+            const unsigned int leftDistance = row[q];
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < blockSize; ++j) {
+                const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + pivotRow[j];
+                if (candidate < row[j]) {
+                    row[j] = candidate;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
-bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Basic sanity checks
-    
-    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
-            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
-            return false;
+void updateRemaining(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRows,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumns,
+                     const Partition rows,
+                     const Partition columns,
+                     const size_t pivotBegin,
+                     const size_t blockSize) noexcept {
+    if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    constexpr size_t COLUMN_TILE = 256;
+    size_t leftEnd = columns.count;
+    size_t rightBegin = columns.count;
+    if (pivotBegin >= columns.begin && pivotBegin < columns.begin + columns.count) {
+        leftEnd = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+        rightBegin = leftEnd + blockSize;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+        if (globalRow >= pivotBegin && globalRow < pivotBegin + blockSize) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        unsigned int* const row = dist.data() + i * columns.count;
+        const unsigned int* const column = pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize;
+        const size_t rangeBegins[2] = {0, rightBegin};
+        const size_t rangeEnds[2] = {leftEnd, columns.count};
+
+        for (int range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+            for (size_t tileBegin = rangeBegins[range]; tileBegin < rangeEnds[range];
+                 tileBegin += COLUMN_TILE) {
+                const size_t tileEnd = std::min(tileBegin + COLUMN_TILE, rangeEnds[range]);
+                for (size_t q = 0; q < blockSize; ++q) {
+                    const unsigned int* const qRow = pivotRows.data() + q * columns.count;
+                    updateContiguous(row, qRow, column[q], tileBegin, tileEnd);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const Partition rows,
+                   const Partition columns,
+                   const int processRow,
+                   const int processColumn,
+                   const int processRows,
+                   const int processColumns,
+                   MPI_Comm rowCommunicator,
+                   MPI_Comm columnCommunicator) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivot;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns;
+    pivot.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * BLOCK_SIZE);
+    pivotRows.reserve(BLOCK_SIZE * columns.count);
+    pivotColumns.reserve(rows.count * BLOCK_SIZE);
+
+    for (size_t pivotBegin = 0; pivotBegin < numNodes;) {
+        const int pivotProcessRow = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processRows);
+        const int pivotProcessColumn = ownerOf(pivotBegin, numNodes, processColumns);
+        const Partition pivotRowPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processRows, pivotProcessRow);
+        const Partition pivotColumnPartition = partitionFor(numNodes, processColumns,
+                                                             pivotProcessColumn);
+        const size_t blockEnd = std::min({pivotBegin + BLOCK_SIZE,
+                                          pivotRowPartition.begin + pivotRowPartition.count,
+                                          pivotColumnPartition.begin + pivotColumnPartition.count,
+                                          numNodes});
+        const size_t blockSize = blockEnd - pivotBegin;
+        pivot.resize(blockSize * blockSize);
+
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow && processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+                const unsigned int* const source = dist.data()
+                                                 + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
+                                                 + localPivotColumn;
+                std::copy_n(source, blockSize, pivot.data() + i * blockSize);
+            }
+            closePivot(pivot, blockSize);
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < blockSize; ++i) {
+                unsigned int* const destination = dist.data()
+                                                + (localPivotRow + i) * columns.count
+                                                + localPivotColumn;
+                std::copy_n(pivot.data() + i * blockSize, blockSize, destination);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const int pivotCount = static_cast<int>(blockSize * blockSize);
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
+            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn,
+                      rowCommunicator);
+            updatePivotRows(dist, pivot, columns, pivotBegin - rows.begin,
+                            pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        }
+        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            MPI_Bcast(pivot.data(), pivotCount, MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessRow,
+                      columnCommunicator);
+            updatePivotColumns(dist, pivot, rows, columns, pivotBegin - columns.begin,
+                               pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        }
+
+        pivotRows.resize(blockSize * columns.count);
+        if (processRow == pivotProcessRow) {
+            const size_t localPivotRow = pivotBegin - rows.begin;
+            if (columns.count != 0) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + localPivotRow * columns.count,
+                            blockSize * columns.count, pivotRows.data());
+            }
+        }
+
+        pivotColumns.resize(rows.count * blockSize);
+        if (processColumn == pivotProcessColumn) {
+            const size_t localPivotColumn = pivotBegin - columns.begin;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count + localPivotColumn,
+                            blockSize, pivotColumns.data() + i * blockSize);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const size_t pivotRowCount = blockSize * columns.count;
+        const size_t pivotColumnCount = rows.count * blockSize;
+        if (pivotRowCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)
+            || pivotColumnCount > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI panel exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        MPI_Ibcast(pivotRows.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRowCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   pivotProcessRow, columnCommunicator, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumns.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumnCount), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   pivotProcessColumn, rowCommunicator, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        updateRemaining(dist, pivotRows, pivotColumns, rows, columns,
+                        pivotBegin, blockSize);
+        pivotBegin = blockEnd;
+    }
+}
+
+void reduceUnsignedSum(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                       std::vector<unsigned int>& result,
+                       const int root,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < local.size()) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(local.size() - offset,
+                                                    static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)));
+        MPI_Reduce(local.data() + offset, result.data() + offset, count, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   MPI_SUM, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<size_t>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                    const size_t numNodes,
+                    const Partition rows,
+                    const Partition columns,
+                    const int rank,
+                    MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    unsigned long long firstBadDiagonal = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numNodes);
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.begin + i;
+        if (globalRow >= columns.begin && globalRow < columns.begin + columns.count
+            && dist[i * columns.count + globalRow - columns.begin] != 0) {
+            firstBadDiagonal = std::min(firstBadDiagonal,
+                                        static_cast<unsigned long long>(globalRow));
+        }
+    }
+
+    unsigned long long globalBadDiagonal = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numNodes);
+    MPI_Reduce(&firstBadDiagonal, &globalBadDiagonal, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
+               MPI_MIN, 0, communicator);
+
+    const size_t sampleSize = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));
+    std::vector<unsigned int> localSamples(2 * sampleSize * numNodes, 0);
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < sampleSize; ++i) {
+        if (columns.count != 0 && i >= rows.begin && i < rows.begin + rows.count) {
+            const unsigned int* const source = dist.data() + (i - rows.begin) * columns.count;
+            std::copy_n(source, columns.count,
+                        localSamples.data() + i * numNodes + columns.begin);
+        }
+        if (i >= columns.begin && i < columns.begin + columns.count) {
+            const size_t localColumn = i - columns.begin;
+            unsigned int* const destination = localSamples.data()
+                                            + (sampleSize + i) * numNodes + rows.begin;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < rows.count; ++k) {
+                destination[k] = dist[k * columns.count + localColumn];
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
-    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                // Check for overflow before addition
-                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
-                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
-                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
-                               i, j, k);
-                        return false;
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> samples(rank == 0 ? localSamples.size() : 0);
+    // MPI requires a valid receive pointer even on some older implementations.
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        samples.resize(localSamples.size());
+    }
+    reduceUnsignedSum(localSamples, samples, 0, communicator);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (globalBadDiagonal != numNodes) {
+            std::printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%llu,%llu] is not zero\n",
+                        globalBadDiagonal, globalBadDiagonal);
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; valid != 0 && i < sampleSize; ++i) {
+            const unsigned int* const sourceRow = samples.data() + i * numNodes;
+            for (size_t j = 0; valid != 0 && j < sampleSize; ++j) {
+                const unsigned int distIJ = sourceRow[j];
+                const unsigned int* const destinationColumn = samples.data()
+                                                                + (sampleSize + j) * numNodes;
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+                    const unsigned int distIK = sourceRow[k];
+                    const unsigned int distKJ = destinationColumn[k];
+                    if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF && distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
+                        std::printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at "
+                                    "[%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", i, j, k);
+                        valid = 0;
+                        break;
                     }
                 }
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
+    return valid != 0;
 }
